 SELECT a.INT_SALESID,a.CHR_SALESNO,FIND_A_CUSTOMER_NAME(b.INT_CUSTOMERID),DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_SALESDATE,'%d-%m-%Y') , a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,a.CHR_PAYMENTSTATUS,a.INT_SALESSTATUS,  FUN_INV_GET_PAYMENTCOMMITMENTCOUNT(a.CHR_SALESNO ),  a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,  FUN_INV_GET_STAFFNAMEBYUSERNAME(a.CHR_USRNAME),  a.CHR_INVOICEBLOCK,a.CHR_SALESTYPE,a.CHR_DELIVERY,DATEDIFF(DATE(a.DAT_COURIERDATE),a.DAT_SALESDATE),a.CHR_CANCEL,    FUN_INV_DIVISION(a.INT_DIVIID) ,a.CHR_CPONUMBER , (NOW() > DATE_ADD(a.DAT_SALESDATE, INTERVAL (SELECT INT_INVOCECHANGES FROM m_inventorysetting WHERE INT_ROWID= 1) DAY ))  dd ,IF(CHR_GST_TYPE='S','State','Central'),CHR_DISCOUNTBILL FROM inv_t_directsales  a ,inv_m_customerinfo b    where  a.INT_CUSTOMERID = b.INT_CUSTOMERID    AND MONTH(a.DAT_SALESDATE) =09 AND YEAR(a.DAT_SALESDATE) =2024 AND  a.CHR_CANCEL  = 'N'    GROUP BY  a.INT_SALESID

','') NARRATION,   SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 1, 50) PROD_DEC1 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 51, 50) PROD_DEC2 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 101, 50) PROD_DEC3 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 151, 50) PROD_DEC4 ,    SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 200, 50) PROD_DEC5 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 201, 50) PROD_DEC6 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 251, 50) PROD_DEC7 ,    FUN_INV_GET_SALES_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(c.CHR_TYPE,c.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_SALESNO) SRNO_DETAILS,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(c.INT_WARRANTY) WARRANTY_DETAILS,   a.CHR_OTHERREF PO_REFERENCE, FIND_A_PAYMENT_TERM(a.INT_PAYMENTTERMID) PAYMENT_TERMS, a.CHR_DESPATCHTHRU DESPATCHED_THRU, a.CHR_DESTINATION DESTINATION,   a.CHR_CONTACTDETAILS CONTACT_PERSON, a.CHR_CONTACTNO CONTACT_NO,   CONCAT(FIND_A_CUSTOMER_NAME(a.INT_CUSTOMERID) )CONSIGNEE_NAME, b.CHR_ADDRESS1 CONSIGNEE_ADDRESS1, b.CHR_ADDRESS2 CONSIGNEE_ADDRESS2,   b.CHR_ADDRESS3 CONSIGNEE_ADDRESS3,  g. CHR_CITYNAME CONSIGNEE_CITY , f.CHR_DISTRICT CONSIGNEE_DISTRICT,  e.CHR_STATENAME CONSIGNEE_STATENAME,    b.INT_PINCODE CONSIGNEE_PINCODE ,IF(a.CHR_GST_TYPE='S','STATE','CENTRAL') GSTTYPE,    IF(a.CHR_SHIPPING ='D', 'Different', 'Same') CONSIGNEEADD   FROM  inv_t_directsales a, inv_m_customerinfo b, inv_t_swapsalesitem c  ,inv_m_tax d , com_m_state  e , com_m_district f, com_m_city g     WHERE  a.INT_CUSTOMERID = b.INT_CUSTOMERID     AND a.CHR_SALESNO =c.CHR_SALESNO    AND c.INT_TAXID = d.INT_TAXID     AND b.INT_STATEID = e.INT_STATEID   AND b.INT_DISTRICTID = f.INT_DISTRICTID   AND b.INT_CITYID = g.INT_CITYID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'    AND a.INT_SALESSTATUS=1     AND a.INT_BRANCHID = 1 AND a.DAT_SALESDATE >= '2024-09-30'  AND a.DAT_SALESDATE <= '2024-09-30'  ORDER BY a.INT_SALESID
